Let's design a rigorous local continuous optimizer with incremental updates to exploit the fact objective is max of pairwise terms. We can implement a "steepest descent with respect to pair transfers" coordinate descent:
For each pair of indices (i,j) with difference m (positive or negative), define transfer δ from higher value to lower value preserving sum. The objective as a function of δ (fixing others) is the maximum over affected quadratic functions. Since only terms involving i or j change; unaffected lags have fixed counts. We can minimize max over δ ∈ [0, Δmax] by scalar optimization (golden/bisection) using exact objective in O(n) per evaluation (or evaluate only affected plus current max). Then accept if improvement. We can do this for pairs at currently largest lags. This is a rigorous coordinate descent (though nonconvex in δ as max of quadratics but scalar can be minimized by sampling or brave new world). It may find exact local optimum where no pair transfer at any lag improves. It generalizes previous local search but with optimal δ rather than fixed steps.

This could squeeze significantly. Number of candidate pairs large. We can focus on pairs associated with top-count lags: if lag m is max, choose i such that h_i > h_{i+m} (for positive m) or h_{i+m} > h_i for negative. Swapping reduces that lag quadratically. We can try many candidates at top lags.

Given h vector, for swap i (a), j (b), δ. For p array = all indices except i,j. hp = h[p]. We need four delta arrays:
lag1 = p - i, delta1 = -δ*(1 - hp)  # i->p
lag2 = i - p, delta2 = δ*hp          # p->i
lag3 = p - j, delta3 = δ*(1 - hp)    # j->p
lag4 = j - p, delta4 = -δ*hp         # p->j
Plus pair i->j delta5 at lag j-i; pair j->i delta6.
We can collect lags in an array length ~4n +2 and use `np.add.at` on a delta_counts array? Per candidate O(n) but vectorized C-level; if n=500, ~2000 length. To evaluate one δ, build `newC = C.copy(); np.add.at(newC, idx+off, vals); newmax = max(newC)`. Building per δ costly.
But we can precompute for a candidate independent of δ: The delta values are linear plus δ^2 for pair i,j. Specifically:
- For lags L in set S, affected count = old + α_L δ + β_L δ^2, where β nonzero only for pair i->j and j->i (δ^2). α_L coefficients. So we can compute max of max_unaffected, max_L (C[L] + α_L δ + β_L δ^2). We can find best δ by evaluating a grid over δ ∈[0, δmax] (e.g., 20 points) using vectorized α,β arrays and current C affected. This is faster than updating per δ.

- For each p≠i,j: L=p-i (α=-1+hp), L=i-p (α=hp), L=p-j (α=1-hp), L=j-p (α=-hp), β=0.

Feasibility projection:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<=1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
           room=1-h; idx=np.where(room>1e-12)[0]
           if len(idx)==0: break
           add=diff/len(idx)
           # but add may exceed room for some; do iterative water fill
           # simpler: h[idx]+=add; clip
           h[idx]+=add
           h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else: ...
    # exact adjust using a few non-saturated
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       idx=np.argsort(h)[?]
       for idx in sorted non-sat: ...
    return h
```
But O(n log n) fine. Need exact sum to 1e-15. We can use a deterministic repair:
After clip, compute diff. If diff >0, need add total diff among indices with h<1. Use greedy: sort indices by h ascending; for each add min(1-h, diff/remaining) until diff=0. If diff<0 sort descending. This preserves [0,1]. Implementation with np.argsort; for many calls maybe expensive. Could optimize with two-pass just distribute to middle? But projection called often (maybe thousands) with n=500; sorting 500 okay but can be overhead. We can use simple iterative clip for gradient steps; for final exact projection, greedy. In local search using full correlate, we can directly choose δ within bounds preserving sum, no projection needed. Start feasible. For gradient, use simple water clip.

`exact_project(h,n)` greedy, use for occasional final and candidate after random.

```
def c5_from_lags_fft(h,n):
    N=1
    need=2*n-1
    while N<need: N<<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft((1.0-h)[::-1],N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:need]
    return float(np.max(conv)* (2.0/n))
```

1. `project_feasible_fast(h,n)` used often:
```
def project_feasible_fast(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
           room=1-h
           tot=room.sum()
           if tot<=1e-15: break
           h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
           tot=h.sum()
           if tot<=1e-15: break
           h += (diff/tot)*h
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact small fix
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.where(h<1.0-1e-12)[0]
       if len(idx): h[idx[0]] = min(1.0, h[idx[0]]+diff); # may overshoot? loop
    else...
    return h
```
But adding diff to room and clipping may not converge if many saturated; but okay. For exact, greedy better:
```
def exact_project(h,n):
   h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
   target=n/2.0
   s=h.sum()
   for _ in range(10):
       s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       if diff>0:
          order=np.argsort(h)
          for idx in order:
             room=1-h[idx]
             if room>0:
                add=min(room,diff)
                h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
                if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
       else:
          order=np.argsort(h, kind='stable')[::-1]
          for idx in order:
             room=h[idx]
             if room>0:
                sub=min(room,-diff)
                h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
                if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
   # final set if tiny still
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)>1e-16:
       idxs=np.where((h>1e-15)&(h<1-1e-15))[0]
       if len(idxs): idx=idxs[len//2]; h[idx]+=diff
   return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
Sort each call, but only initial/resolution/occasional. Fast projection used in gradient.

```
        improved=False

            m=abs(lag)

        # To reduce top lag specifically, prefer candidates that align orientation with max lag.
```

```
def resample(h,n_target):
   if n_target==len(h): return h
   x0=np.linspace(0,2,len(h)); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n_target)
   h1=np.interp(x1,x0,h)
   h1=exact_project(h1,n_target)
   return h1
```

Let's write `smooth_max_value(counts,alpha)` stable:
```
def smooth_max_value(c,alpha):
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   m=c.max()
   s=alpha*(c-m)
   mx=s.max()
   if mx>500: val=m+(mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))))/alpha
   else: val=m+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))/alpha
   return float(val)
```
But for SLSQP, if counts raw or with dx? Use raw* dx. In gradient need weights.

Let's design `transfer_local_search` in detail.
```
def transfer_local_search(h, n, deadline, rng, binary=False):
    h = exact_project(h,n).copy()
    c = c5_exact(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    dx=2.0/n
    no_improve=0
    round_num=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        round_num +=1
        counts_raw = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')  # no dx
        ccur = float(np.max(counts_raw))*dx
        if ccur < best_c - 1e-13:
             best_c=ccur; best_h=h.copy()
        # if binary at start and ccur ...
        maxcnt = counts_raw.max()
        # top indexes: use top 8
        topk = min(8, 2*n-1)
        top_idx = np.argsort(counts_raw)[-topk:][::-1]
        # also include near max within 5%?
        near = np.where(counts_raw >= maxcnt - max(1e-12, 0.05*maxcnt))[0]
        # combine unique limited 10
        candidate_lags = list(dict.fromkeys(list(top_idx)+list(near)))[:10] but dict top-level? okay no closure. Use helper `unique_lags`.
        improved=False
        for lag_idx in candidate_lags:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            lag = lag_idx - (n-1)
            m=abs(lag)
            if m==0 or m>=n: continue
            cands=[]
            # For targeted lag
            for i in range(n-m):
                j=i+m
                if lag>0:
                   if h[i] > h[j] + 1e-8: cands.append((i,j,h[i]-h[j]))
                else:
                   if h[j] > h[i] + 1e-8: cands.append((j,i,h[j]-h[i]))
            # Add opposite if too few
            if len(cands)<5:
               if lag>0:
                   for i ... if h[j]>h[i]+... cands.append((j,i,...))
               else:
                   if h[i]>h[j]+... cands.append((i,j,...))
            if not cands: continue
            # Sort by diff descending, take top 12 plus 12 random
            cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[2], reverse=True) # lambda prohibited! Use index sort.
```
No lambdas. Use `order=np.argsort([-c for ...])` but need list. We can create arrays:
```

   selected=np.unique(np.concatenate((order[:12],rand_idx)))
else: selected=order
for si in selected[:20]: i=src[si]; j=dst[si]
```

```
def candidate_best_delta(h,n,i,j,base_c):
    a=h[i]; b=h[j]
    dmax=min(a,1.0-b)
    if dmax <= 1e-10: return None
    # if a <= b+1e-10: dmax? maybe moving from lower to higher not reduce max; but could reduce other lag? For targeted, a>b.
    fracs=[0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0]
    # add delta0 for targeted? no
    best_delta=None; best_c=base_c
    for f in fracs:
       d=dmax*f
       if d<=1e-12: continue
       h[i]=a-d; h[j]=b+d
       c2=c5_eval(h,n)
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-13:
           best_c=c2; best_delta=d
    h[i]=a; h[j]=b
    return best_delta,best_c
```

```
    m=abs(lag)

    if len(src)==0: add opposite...
    if len(src)> max_total: select top by diff plus random
    return np.array(src), np.array(dst), np.array(diff)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

TARGET_SUM_TOL = 1e-14

def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)
```
If n=0? no.
```
def c5_fft(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; need=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<need: N <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)[:need]
    val=float(np.max(conv))*dx
    if val<0 and val>-1e-14: val=0.0
    return val
```
But if h length not power? OK. `irfft` with n=N returns length N; ensure input `fa*fb` length N//2+1.
For n small, direct.

```
def top_score(counts,n):
    counts=np.asarray(counts,float)
    k=min(20,len(counts))
    if k == len(counts): return float(counts.sum())
    idx=np.argpartition(counts, -k)[-k:]
    return float(np.sum(counts[idx]))
```

```
def fast_project(h,n):
    h=np.clip(h.copy(),0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(60):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*room
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h += (diff/tot)*h
        h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    return exact_project(h,n)
```
This calls exact_project sorting; maybe too expensive if used in gradient each step. But we may not use gradient many. Could just return h after fast clips; final exact. `exact_project` robust:
```
def exact_project(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(4):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
        if diff>0:
            order=np.argsort(h)
            for idx in order:
                room=1.0-h[idx]
                if room>0:
                    add=room if room<diff else diff
                    if add>0:
                        h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
                        if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
        else:
            neg=-diff
            order=np.argsort(h)
            # take descending
            for idx in order[::-1]:
                room=h[idx]
                if room>0:
                    sub=room if room<neg else neg
                    if sub>0:
                        h[idx]-=sub; neg-=sub
                        if neg<1e-15: break
            diff = -(neg) # not use
    # final tiny fix
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
        idxs=np.where((h>1e-15)&(h<1.0-1e-15))[0]
        if idxs.size:
            idx=idxs[len(idxs)//2]
            h[idx] += diff
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
But in subtract loop, `diff` not updated; at end recompute. OK.

`binarize`:

```
def binarize(h,n):
    xb=np.zeros(n)
    k=n//2
    idx=np.argsort(h)[::-1][:k]
    xb[idx]=1.0
    return xb
```

```
def resample_linear(h,n_target):
    if n_target==len(h): return exact_project(h,n_target)
    n=len(h)
    x0=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    x1=(np.arange(n_target)+0.5)*(2.0/n_target)
    hn=np.interp(x1,x0,h)
    return exact_project(hn,n_target)

def resample_nearest(h,n_target):
    if ...
    n=len(h)
    if n_target % n ==0 or n % n_target ==0? use repeat? Simpler centers.
    x0=...
    idx=np.searchsorted(x0, x1)
    idx=np.clip(idx,0,n-1)
    # choose nearest by comparing x1 to x0[idx-1],x0[idx]
    left=np.clip(idx-1,0,n-1)
    d0=np.abs(x1-x0[idx]); d1=np.abs(x1-x0[left]); idx=np.where(d1<d0,left,idx)
    hn=h[idx]
    return exact_project(hn,n_target)
```

```
def select_indices_by_diff(src,diff,max_count,rng):
   if len(src)==0: return np.empty(0,int)
   dnp=np.asarray(diff,float)
   order=np.argsort(-dnp).astype(int)
   if len(order) <= max_count: return order
   base=order[:max_count]
   if rng is not None and len(order) > 2*max_count:
       extra=rng.choice(len(order), max_count, replace=False)
       base=np.unique(np.concatenate((base,extra)))
   return base[:max_count]
```

```
def select_indices_mixed(src,diff,max_count,rng):
   if len(src)==0: return np.empty(0,int)
   dnp=np.asarray(diff,float); order=np.argsort(-dnp)
   if len(order) <= 2*max_count: return order

   if rng is not None:
      extra=rng.choice(len(order), max_count, replace=False)
      base=np.unique(np.concatenate((base,extra)))
```

```
def random_transfer_search(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=exact_project(h,n).copy(); c=c5_eval(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    T=0.004
    use_fft = n>=900
    min_step=1e-5
    while time.time()<deadline:
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       a=h[i]; b=h[j]
       if rng.random()<0.8:
          if a<=b: i,j=b,a? Wait if moving high to low: if a>b, source=i dest=j else i,j=j,i; a=h[i];b=h[j]
       else: random direction
       dmax=min(h[i],1-h[j])
       if dmax<min_step: continue
       d=rng.choice([0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0])*dmax
       h[i]-=d; h[j]+=d
       c2=c5_eval(h,n)
       if c2 < c - 1e-12 or (abs(c2-c)<1e-10 and rng.random()<0.2) or rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0,c2-c)/T):
           c=c2
           if c2 < best_c -1e-12:
              best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
           if c2 <= best_c +1e-12: # maybe keep current if same? We can keep current for search; best_h only lower.
              pass
       else:
           h[i]+=d; h[j]-=d
       T=max(1e-6,T*0.9995)
       # periodically ensure sum
    # final exact
    c=c5_exact(h)
    if c < best_c: best_h=h; best_c=c
    return best_h,best_c
```

SLSQP functions: Write smooth max/grad. Need ensure no reference to undefined.
```
def smooth_max_value(c,alpha):
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   if c.size==0: return 0.0
   m=c.max()
   s=alpha*(c-m)
   mx=s.max()
   if mx>500:
      return float(m+(mx+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-mx))))/alpha)
   return float(m+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))/alpha)
```
If alpha=0? no.
```
def smooth_max_weights(c,alpha):
   c=np.asarray(c,float); m=c.max(); s=alpha*(c-m); mx=s.max()
   w = np.exp(s-mx) if mx>500 else np.exp(s)
   w=w/w.sum(); return w
```
`_lag_gradient` as previous. For lag positive:
```
grad[m:n] += 1-h[0:n-m]
grad[0:n-m] -= h[m:n]
```
Check sign: For c_l positive sum h_i(1-h_{i+m}). derivative wrt h_i first: 1-h_{i+m}, for i 0..n-m-1 -> grad[0:n-m] += 1-h[m:n]. derivative wrt h_{i+m}: -h_i, indices m..n-1 -> grad[m:n] -= h[0:n-m]. My previous had grad[m:n]+=1-h[0:n-m] and grad[0:n-m]-=h[m:n], which seems wrong! Let's correct.
For lag l>0: D = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-l} h_i (1 - h_{i+l}).
- grad[i] (i first) for i=0..n-1-l: (1 - h[i+l]) => add to grad[0:n-l] += 1 - h[l:n].
- grad[i+l] (i second) for i=0..n-1-l: -h_i => add to grad[l:n] += -h[0:n-l].
So correct:
```
if lag>0:
    grad[0:n-m] += (1.0 - h[m:n])
    grad[m:n] += (-1.0) * h[0:n-m]
else lag<0: D=∑_{i=m}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-m}) (directed from i to i-m). Let j=i-m 0..n-m-1: h_{j+m}(1-h_j).
- grad[j+m] (first): 1 - h_j -> grad[m:n] += 1-h[0:n-m]
- grad[j] (second): -h_{j+m} -> grad[0:n-m] += -h[m:n]
```
This matches previous? Previous for lag>0 had inverted; likely bug in previous code maybe. Need correct.
Let's verify with n=2, lag=1: D=h0(1-h1). grad0=1-h1, grad1=-h0. Our positive: grad[0:1]+=1-h[1:2]; grad[1:2]+=-h[0:1] yes. lag=-1: D=h1(1-h0). grad1=1-h0, grad0=-h1. negative: grad[1:2]+=1-h[0:1]; grad[0:1]+=-h[1:2] yes.
Good. Previous may have had bug causing less improvement? Let's use correct.

`grad_smooth_max`:
```
def grad_smooth_max(h,n,alpha,top_idx=None):
   counts=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')* (2/n)
   if top_idx is None:
      w=smooth_max_weights(counts,alpha)
   else:
      cactive=counts[top_idx]; w=smooth_max_weights(cactive,alpha); wfull=zeros; wfull[top_idx]=w
   g=np.zeros(n); dx=2/n
   for k in range(len(wfull)):
       wk=wfull[k]
       if wk<1e-10: continue
       lag=k-(n-1)
       g += wk * _lag_gradient(h,n,lag)
   return g*dx
```
If top_idx not None, loops over all lags but skip empty; O(2n) loops. Could optimize active only by index list, but fine for n=500.
`_active_smooth_obj(x,n,top_idx,alpha)`:
```
def _active_smooth_obj(x,n,top_idx,alpha):
    counts=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')*(2/n)
    if top_idx is None: c=counts
    else: c=counts[top_idx]
    return smooth_max_value(c,alpha)
```
`_active_smooth_jac(x,n,top_idx,alpha)`: return grad_smooth_max...
`_sum_con(x,n): return float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)`, jac ones.
`slsqp_active(h,n,top_idx,alpha,maxiter)`:
```
def slsqp_active(h,n,top_idx,alpha,maxiter=200):
   h=exact_project(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)]
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_con,'jac':_sum_con_jac,'args':(n,)}]
   try:
      res=minimize(_active_smooth_obj,h,args=(n,top_idx,alpha),jac=_active_smooth_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      if res.x is not None and len(res.x)==n:
         return exact_project(res.x,n)
   except Exception:
      pass
   return h
```
No closure. Good.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf')

    except Exception:

    c0=c5_exact(h0)
    best_h=h0; best_c=c0

       alloc=min(120, max(30,(deadline-time.time())*0.2))

          hb2,cb2=transfer_local_search(hb,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
          if c<best: update
          # also random SA on binary maybe
    # 3 random SA
    if time.time() < deadline-80:
       alloc=min(80,...)
       hr,cr=random_transfer_search(best_h,n,time+alloc,rng)
    # 4 SLSQP

       for top_m in [10,20]:

          counts=lag_counts(best_h); top=np.argsort(counts)[-top_m:]
          hn=slsqp_active(best_h,n,top,100000,200)
          cn=c5_exact(hn); update
    # 5 high res 1000
    for n_new in [1000, 1400]:
       if time.time() > deadline-30: break
       remaining=deadline-time.time()
       alloc=min(220, remaining*0.25)
       if alloc<60: break
       h_new=resample_linear(best_h,n_new)
       h_new,c_new=transfer_local_search(h_new,n_new,time.time()+alloc*0.7,rng)
       update
       # nearest maybe

          h_near=resample_nearest(best_h,n_new)
          h_near,c_near=transfer_local_search(h_near,n_new,time.time()+min(60, ...),rng)
          update
       if time.time() < deadline-90:
          hr,cr=random_transfer_search(h_best new, n_new, time+min(40,..),rng)
    # 6 multistart low res random smooth? if time
    if time.time() < deadline-120:
       n_start=max(200, min(400,len(best_h)//2))
       for r in range(3): ... generate Fourier/random, local short, update
    # final polish best with small time
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < deadline-3:
       n=len(best_h)
       hn,cn=transfer_local_search(best_h,n,deadline-1,rng)
       if c<best: update
       hn=exact_project(hn,n); cn=c5_exact(hn); update
    if best_h is None: fallback
    best_h=exact_project(best_h,n); best_c=c5_exact(best_h)
    return best_h,best_c,n
```

Random smooth starts: Let's implement a function `generate_fourier_h(n,rng,sigma)` top-level.
```
def generate_fourier_h(n,rng,sigma):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   h=np.full(n,0.5)
   for m in [1,2,3,4,5,6]:
      a=rng.uniform(-sigma,sigma); b=rng.uniform(-sigma,sigma)
      h += a*np.cos(m*np.pi*x) + b*np.sin(m*np.pi*x)
   # add low linear zero mean
   h += sigma*rng.uniform(0,1)*(x-1)
   return exact_project(h,n)
```

```
def perturb_h(h,rng,scale):
```

Full code draft for transfer:
```
def transfer_local_search(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=exact_project(h,n).copy()
    dx=2.0/n
    counts=lag_counts(h)
    ccur=float(counts.max())*dx
    cur_score=top_score(counts,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=ccur; best_score=cur_score
    use_score = n <= 700
    top_lags = 12 if n<=700 else 7
    max_target = 10 if n<=700 else 6
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if use_score:
            counts=lag_counts(h)
            ccur=float(counts.max())*dx
            cur_score=top_score(counts,n)
        else:
            # still need counts for lags; use direct
            counts=lag_counts(h)
            ccur=float(counts.max())*dx
            cur_score=top_score(counts,n)
        if ccur < best_c - 1e-13 or (abs(ccur-best_c)<1e-12 and cur_score<best_score-1e-8):
            best_c=ccur; best_h=h.copy(); best_score=cur_score
        maxcnt=float(counts.max())
        if maxcnt <= 1e-14:
            break
        order=np.argsort(counts)
        top_idx=order[-top_lags:][::-1]
        near_idx=np.where(counts >= maxcnt - max(1e-12,0.10*maxcnt))[0]
        cand_lags=np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx,near_idx)))
        cand_lags=cand_lags[:top_lags+5]
        improved=False
        for lag_idx in cand_lags:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            lag=int(lag_idx-(n-1))
            m=abs(lag)
            if m==0 or m>=n: continue
            src,dst=build_candidates(h,n,lag,targeted=True,max_target=max_target,max_opp=max_target//2,rng=rng)
            for slot in range(len(src)):
                i=src[slot]; j=dst[slot]
                res=evaluate_move(h,n,i,j,ccur,cur_score,use_score)
                if res is not None:
                    d,bc,bs=res
                    # apply using current a,b? evaluate left h unchanged. Need fetch a,b before? It restores.
                    a=h[i]; b=h[j]; h[i]=a-d; h[j]=b+d
                    ccur=bc; cur_score=bs; best_c=min(best_c,bc); if score update
                    improved=True; break
            if improved or time.time()>deadline: break
        if improved:
            no_improve=0
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            # Try opposite pass
            if no_improve < 10:
               for lag_idx in cand_lags[:3]: ...
                   build targeted=False ...
                   evaluate; accept; improve
               no_improve = 0 if improved else no_improve
            if no_improve > 12:
               break
    c_final=c5_exact(h)
    if c_final < best_c - 1e-13 or (abs(c_final-best_c)<1e-12 and top_score(lag_counts(h),n) < best_score):
       best_h=h; best_c=c_final
    return best_h,best_c
```

`select_indices_mixed` if list length 0: return empty int.
```
def select_indices_mixed(src,diff,max_count,rng):
    if len(src)==0 or max_count<=0: return np.empty(0,dtype=int)
    dnp=np.asarray(diff,float)
    order=np.argsort(-dnp).astype(int)
    if len(order) <= 2*max_count:
       return order
    base=order[:max_count]
    if rng is not None:
       try: extra=rng.choice(len(order), max_count, replace=False)
       except Exception: extra=order[max_count:2*max_count]
       base=np.unique(np.concatenate((base,extra)))
    base=np.unique(base).astype(int)
    if len(base)>2*max_count: base=base[:2*max_count]
    return base
```
Note if rng.choice returns unsorted duplicates removed; okay.

`evaluate_move` for use_score True: It recomputes counts direct for every delta. Could be a bit slow but allows score. For n≤700. Good.
```
def evaluate_move(h,n,i,j,cur_c,cur_score,use_score):
    a=float(h[i]); b=float(h[j])
    dmax=min(a,1.0-b)
    if dmax <= 1e-12: return None
    fracs = (0.1,0.25,0.4,0.6,0.8,1.0) if n <= 900 else (0.15,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0)
    best_d=None; best_c=cur_c; best_s=cur_score
    for frac in fracs:
       d=dmax*frac
       if d <= 1e-13: continue
       h[i]=a-d; h[j]=b+d
       if use_score:
          counts=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
          c2=float(counts.max())*(2.0/n)
          s2=top_score(counts,n)
       else:
          c2=c5_eval(h,n); s2=cur_score
       if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
          best_c=c2; best_d=d; best_s=s2
       elif use_score and c2 <= best_c + 1e-10 and s2 < best_s - 1e-8:
          if best_d is None or c2 <= best_c + 1e-12:
             best_c=c2; best_d=d; best_s=s2
       h[i]=a; h[j]=b
    if best_d is None: return None
    return (best_d,best_c,best_s)
```
If use_score False, initial best_s=cur_score. If c2 lower, accept. If c2 equal but s2 same, no.
Potential issue: If c2 lower for one delta, then later delta c2 slightly higher but score much lower, we ignore. Good because primary max.

```
def random_fourier_h(n,rng,sigma=0.2):
   x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   h=np.full(n,0.5)
   for m in range(1,7):
      h += sigma*rng.random()*np.cos(np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      h += sigma*rng.random()*np.sin(np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
   return exact_project(h,n)
```

Let's write `exact_project` more carefully to guarantee sum exact. Implementation:
```
def exact_project(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(8):
        s=h.sum()
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            order=np.argsort(h)
            for idx in order:
                if diff <= 1e-15: break
                room=1.0-h[idx]
                if room > 0.0:
                    add=diff if diff < room else room
                    h[idx]+=add
                    diff-=add
        else:
            neg=-diff
            order=np.argsort(h)
            for idx in order[::-1]:
                if neg <= 1e-15: break
                avail=h[idx]
                if avail > 0.0:
                    sub=neg if neg < avail else avail
                    h[idx]-=sub
                    neg-=sub
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       idxs=np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0-1e-14))[0]
       if idxs.size:
          idx=idxs[len(idxs)//2]
          h[idx]+=diff
    return np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
```
If final adjust pushes out and clip changes sum; but diff tiny. OK.

`fast_project` maybe not used except maybe in random? We can skip. `perturb` uses exact.

In `random_transfer_search`, when moving high to low after choosing i,j, if a<=b, we swap i,j. But if we wanted random direction, handle separately.
```
def random_transfer_search(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=exact_project(h,n).copy()
    c=c5_exact(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    T=0.004
    use_fft = n>=900
    restart_count=0
    while time.time()<deadline:
       i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       a=h[i]; b=h[j]
       if rng.random() < 0.85:
          if a > b:
             src=dst=i; dst=j
          elif b > a:
             src=i; dst=j
          else: continue
       else:
          if rng.random()<0.5: src=i; dst=j
          else: src=j; dst=i
       a=h[src]; b=h[dst]
       dmax=min(a,1.0-b)
       if dmax<1e-4: continue
       steps=(0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)
       d=dmax*steps[rng.randint(len(steps))]
       h[src]-=d; h[dst]+=d
       if use_fft: c2=c5_fft(h)
       else: c2=c5_exact(h)
       accept=False
       if c2 < c - 1e-12:
          accept=True
       else:
          diff=max(0.0,c2-c)
          if c2 < best_c + 0.004 and diff < 10.0*T:
             accept = (rng.random() < np.exp(-diff/T))
       if accept:
          c=c2
          if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
             best_c=c2; best_h=h.copy()
       else:
          h[src]+=d; h[dst]-=d
       if c > best_c + 0.005:
          h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c
          restart_count+=1
          if restart_count>10:
             T=max(1e-5,T*0.9)
       T=max(1e-5,T*0.9995)
    c_final=c5_exact(h)
    if c_final < best_c - 1e-12:
       best_h=h; best_c=c_final
    return best_h,best_c
```
If c current becomes worse but not restarted immediately unless >best+0.005. Good.

In final, if h current same max but lower? not update; fine.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    try: total=float(budget_s)
    except: total=1100.0
    deadline=t0+max(1.0,total)-3.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=float('inf')
    # load parent
    h0=None; n0=500
    try:
       raw=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
       if raw.size>=20:
          h0=raw.copy()
          n0=len(h0)
          if n0%2==1:
             n0-=1; h0=h0[:n0]
    except Exception:
       h0=None
    if h0 is None:
       n0=500; h0=np.full(n0,0.5)
    h0=exact_project(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0; best_c=c5_exact(h0)
    n=len(best_h)
    # Stage 1
    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
       alloc=min(200.0, max(30.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.25))

          hh,cc=transfer_local_search(best_h,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)
          cc=c5_exact(hh)
          if cc<best_c: best_h=hh; best_c=cc; n=len(hh)
    # Binary
    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
       hb=binarize(best_h,n)
       cb=c5_exact(hb)
       if cb<best_c: best_h=hb; best_c=cb
       alloc=min(120.0, max(25.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.20))
       if alloc>25:
          hh,cc=transfer_local_search(hb,n,time.time()+alloc,rng)

    # Random SA

    # SLSQP

          # quick local on SLSQP result

             cl=c5_exact(hl)
             if cl<best_c: best_h=hl; best_c=cn? Wait if update.
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def c5_exact(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def c5_fft(h):
    n = len(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft((1.0 - h)[::-1], N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:need]
    val = float(np.max(conv)) * dx
    if val < 0.0 and val > -1e-14:
        val = 0.0
    return val


def c5_eval(h, n):
    if n >= 900:
        return c5_fft(h)
    return c5_exact(h)


def lag_counts(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def top_score(counts, n):
    counts = np.asarray(counts, dtype=float)
    k = min(20, len(counts))
    if k <= 0:
        return 0.0
    if k == len(counts):
        return float(np.sum(counts))
    idx = np.argpartition(counts, -k)[-k:]
    return float(np.sum(counts[idx]))


def exact_project(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(8):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            for idx in order:
                if diff <= 1e-15:
                    break
                room = 1.0 - h[idx]
                if room > 0.0:
                    add = diff if diff < room else room
                    if add > 0.0:
                        h[idx] += add
                        diff -= add
        else:
            neg = -diff
            order = np.argsort(h)
            for idx in order[::-1]:
                if neg <= 1e-15:
                    break
                avail = h[idx]
                if avail > 0.0:
                    sub = neg if neg < avail else avail
                    if sub > 0.0:
                        h[idx] -= sub
                        neg -= sub
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
        idxs = np.where((h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14))[0]
        if idxs.size:
            idx = idxs[len(idxs) // 2]
            h[idx] += diff
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def binarize(h, n):
    xb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0:
        idx = np.argsort(h)[::-1][:k]
        xb[idx] = 1.0
    return xb


def resample_linear(h, n_target):
    if n_target == len(h):
        return exact_project(h, n_target)
    n = len(h)
    x0 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x1 = (np.arange(n_target) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_target)
    hn = np.interp(x1, x0, h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
    return exact_project(hn, n_target)


def resample_nearest(h, n_target):
    if n_target == len(h):
        return exact_project(h, n_target)
    n = len(h)
    x0 = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x1 = (np.arange(n_target) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_target)
    idx = np.searchsorted(x0, x1, side='left')
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n - 1)
    left = np.clip(idx - 1, 0, n - 1)
    d0 = np.abs(x1 - x0[idx])
    d1 = np.abs(x1 - x0[left])
    idx = np.where(d1 < d0, left, idx)
    hn = h[idx]
    return exact_project(hn, n_target)


def perturb_h(h, rng, scale):
    hp = h + rng.normal(0.0, scale, h.size)
    return exact_project(hp, len(h))


def select_indices_mixed(src, diff, max_count, rng):
    if len(src) == 0 or max_count <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int)
    dnp = np.asarray(diff, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(-dnp).astype(int)
    if len(order) <= 2 * max_count:
        return order
    base = order[:max_count]
    if rng is not None:
        try:
            extra = rng.choice(len(order), max_count, replace=False)
        except Exception:
            extra = order[max_count:2 * max_count]
        base = np.unique(np.concatenate((base, extra))).astype(int)
    else:
        base = np.unique(base).astype(int)
    if len(base) > 2 * max_count:
        base = base[:2 * max_count]
    return base


def build_candidates(h, n, lag, targeted=True, max_target=10, max_opp=5, rng=None):
    m = abs(int(lag))
    if m <= 0 or m >= n:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=int), np.empty(0, dtype=int)

    src = []
    dst = []
    diff = []

    opp_src = []
    opp_dst = []
    opp_diff = []

    if lag > 0:
        for i in range(n - m):
            j = i + m
            if h[i] > h[j] + 1e-9:
                src.append(i)
                dst.append(j)
                diff.append(float(h[i] - h[j]))
            elif h[j] > h[i] + 1e-9:
                opp_src.append(j)
                opp_dst.append(i)
                opp_diff.append(float(h[j] - h[i]))
    else:
        for i in range(n - m):
            j = i + m
            if h[j] > h[i] + 1e-9:
                src.append(j)
                dst.append(i)
                diff.append(float(h[j] - h[i]))
            elif h[i] > h[j] + 1e-9:
                opp_src.append(i)
                opp_dst.append(j)
                opp_diff.append(float(h[i] - h[j]))

    if not targeted:
        src = src + opp_src
        dst = dst + opp_dst
        diff = diff + opp_diff

    out_src = []
    out_dst = []

    if len(src) > 0:
        selected = select_indices_mixed(src, diff, max_target, rng)
        for it in selected:
            out_src.append(src[int(it)])
            out_dst.append(dst[int(it)])

    if targeted and len(out_src) < max_target and len(opp_src) > 0:
        selected = select_indices_mixed(opp_src, opp_diff, max(1, max_opp), rng)
        for it in selected:
            out_src.append(opp_src[int(it)])
            out_dst.append(opp_dst[int(it)])

    return np.asarray(out_src, dtype=int), np.asarray(out_dst, dtype=int)


def evaluate_move(h, n, i, j, cur_c, cur_score, use_score):
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    dmax = min(a, 1.0 - b)
    if dmax <= 1e-12:
        return None

    if n <= 900:
        fracs = (0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.6, 0.8, 1.0)
    else:
        fracs = (0.15, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)

    best_d = None
    best_c = cur_c
    best_s = cur_score

    for frac in fracs:
        d = dmax * float(frac)
        if d <= 1e-13:
            continue
        h[i] = a - d
        h[j] = b + d
        if use_score:
            counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            c2 = float(np.max(counts)) * (2.0 / n)
            s2 = top_score(counts, n)
        else:
            c2 = c5_eval(h, n)
            s2 = cur_score

        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c2
            best_d = d
            best_s = s2
        elif use_score and c2 <= best_c + 1e-10 and s2 < best_s - 1e-8:
            if best_d is None or c2 <= best_c + 1e-12:
                best_c = c2
                best_d = d
                best_s = s2

        h[i] = a
        h[j] = b

    if best_d is None:
        return None
    return (best_d, best_c, best_s)


def transfer_local_search(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = exact_project(h, n).copy()
    dx = 2.0 / n
    counts = lag_counts(h)
    ccur = float(np.max(counts)) * dx
    cur_score = top_score(counts, n)

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = ccur
    best_score = cur_score

    use_score = n <= 700
    top_lags = 12 if n <= 700 else 7
    max_target = 10 if n <= 700 else 6
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        counts = lag_counts(h)
        ccur = float(np.max(counts)) * dx
        cur_score = top_score(counts, n)

        if ccur < best_c - 1e-13 or (abs(ccur - best_c) < 1e-12 and cur_score < best_score - 1e-8):
            best_c = ccur
            best_score = cur_score
            best_h = h.copy()

        maxcnt = float(np.max(counts))
        if maxcnt <= 1e-14:
            break

        order = np.argsort(counts)
        top_idx = order[-top_lags:][::-1]
        near_idx = np.where(counts >= maxcnt - max(1e-12, 0.10 * maxcnt))[0]
        cand_lags = np.unique(np.concatenate((top_idx, near_idx)))
        cand_lags = cand_lags[:top_lags + 6]

        improved = False

        for pass_no in (0, 1):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            targeted = (pass_no == 0)
            lag_limit = len(cand_lags) if targeted else min(3, len(cand_lags))

            for lag_idx in cand_lags[:lag_limit]:
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                lag = int(lag_idx - (n - 1))
                m = abs(lag)

                if m == 0:
                    for _ in range(25 if targeted else 8):
                        i = rng.randint(n)
                        j = rng.randint(n)
                        if i == j:
                            continue
                        hi = float(h[i])
                        hj = float(h[j])
                        if hi <= 1e-6 or hi >= 1.0 - 1e-6:
                            continue
                        if hj <= 1e-6 or hj >= 1.0 - 1e-6:
                            continue
                        order_try = (i, j) if hi >= hj else (j, i)
                        for (src_idx, dst_idx) in (order_try, (dst_idx, src_idx)):
                            res = evaluate_move(h, n, src_idx, dst_idx, ccur, cur_score, use_score)
                            if res is not None:
                                d, bc, bs = res
                                a = float(h[src_idx])
                                b = float(h[dst_idx])
                                h[src_idx] = a - d
                                h[dst_idx] = b + d
                                ccur = bc
                                cur_score = bs
                                if ccur < best_c - 1e-13 or (abs(ccur - best_c) < 1e-12 and cur_score < best_score - 1e-8):
                                    best_c = ccur
                                    best_score = cur_score
                                    best_h = h.copy()
                                improved = True
                                break
                        if improved:
                            break
                    if improved:
                        break
                    continue

                src_arr, dst_arr = build_candidates(
                    h, n, lag,
                    targeted=targeted,
                    max_target=max_target,
                    max_opp=max(1, max_target // 2),
                    rng=rng
                )
                for slot in range(len(src_arr)):
                    if time.time() > deadline:
                        break
                    i = int(src_arr[slot])
                    j = int(dst_arr[slot])
                    res = evaluate_move(h, n, i, j, ccur, cur_score, use_score)
                    if res is not None:
                        d, bc, bs = res
                        a = float(h[i])
                        b = float(h[j])
                        h[i] = a - d
                        h[j] = b + d
                        ccur = bc
                        cur_score = bs
                        if ccur < best_c - 1e-13 or (abs(ccur - best_c) < 1e-12 and cur_score < best_score - 1e-8):
                            best_c = ccur
                            best_score = cur_score
                            best_h = h.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 18:
                break

    c_final = c5_exact(h)
    if c_final < best_c - 1e-13:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_c = c_final
    elif abs(c_final - best_c) < 1e-12:
        s_final = top_score(lag_counts(h), n)
        if s_final < best_score - 1e-8:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c_final
            best_score = s_final

    return best_h, best_c


def random_transfer_search(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h = exact_project(h, n).copy()
    c = c5_exact(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c
    T = 0.004
    use_fft = n >= 900
    steps = (0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
    restarts = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        i = rng.randint(n)
        j = rng.randint(n)
        if i == j:
            continue

        a = float(h[i])
        b = float(h[j])

        if rng.random() < 0.85:
            if a > b + 1e-8:
                src, dst = i, j
            elif b > a + 1e-8:
                src, dst = j, i
            else:
                src, dst = (i, j) if rng.random() < 0.5 else (j, i)
        else:
            src, dst = (i, j) if rng.random() < 0.5 else (j, i)

        a = float(h[src])
        b = float(h[dst])
        dmax = min(a, 1.0 - b)
        if dmax < 1e-4:
            continue

        d = dmax * float(steps[rng.randint(len(steps))])
        h[src] = a - d
        h[dst] = b + d

        if use_fft:
            c2 = c5_fft(h)
        else:
            c2 = c5_exact(h)

        accept = False
        if c2 < c - 1e-12:
            accept = True
        else:
            diff = max(0.0, c2 - c)
            if c2 < best_c + 0.004 and diff < 10.0 * T:
                accept = (rng.random() < np.exp(-diff / T))

        if accept:
            c = c2
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h.copy()
        else:
            h[src] = a
            h[dst] = b

        if c > best_c + 0.005:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c
            restarts += 1
            if restarts % 5 == 0:
                T = max(1e-5, T * 0.85)

        T = max(1e-5, T * 0.9995)

    c_final = c5_exact(h)
    if c_final < best_c - 1e-12:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_c = c_final

    return best_h, best_c


def smooth_max_value(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    m = float(np.max(c))
    s = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(s))
    if mx > 500.0:
        return float(m + (mx + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s - mx)))) / alpha)
    return float(m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s))) / alpha)


def smooth_max_weights(c, alpha):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    s = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(s))
    if mx > 500.0:
        w = np.exp(s - mx)
    else:
        w = np.exp(s)
    w = w / float(np.sum(w))
    return w


def _lag_gradient(h, n, lag):
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = abs(int(lag))
    if m == 0:
        grad[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
        return grad
    if m >= n:
        return grad
    if lag > 0:
        grad[0:n - m] += (1.0 - h[m:n])
        grad[m:n] += (-1.0) * h[0:n - m]
    else:
        grad[m:n] += (1.0 - h[0:n - m])
        grad[0:n - m] += (-1.0) * h[m:n]
    return grad


def grad_smooth_max(h, n, alpha, top_idx=None):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    counts = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx
    if top_idx is None:
        w = smooth_max_weights(counts, alpha)
    else:
        cactive = counts[np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)]
        w = smooth_max_weights(cactive, alpha)
        wfull = np.zeros(len(counts), dtype=float)
        wfull[np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)] = w
        w = wfull

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for k in range(len(w)):
        wk = float(w[k])
        if wk < 1e-10:
            continue
        lag = k - (n - 1)
        g += wk * _lag_gradient(h, n, lag)
    return g * dx


def _active_smooth_obj(x, n, top_idx, alpha):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    counts = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    if top_idx is None:
        cc = counts
    else:
        cc = counts[np.asarray(top_idx, dtype=int)]
    return smooth_max_value(cc, alpha)


def _active_smooth_jac(x, n, top_idx, alpha):
    return grad_smooth_max(x, n, alpha, top_idx)


def _sum_con(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)


def _sum_con_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def slsqp_active(h, n, top_idx, alpha, maxiter=80):
    h = exact_project(h, n).copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_con, 'jac': _sum_con_jac, 'args': (n,)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _active_smooth_obj,
            h,
            args=(n, top_idx, alpha),
            jac=_active_smooth_jac,
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12}
        )
        if res.x is not None and len(res.x) == n:
            return exact_project(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1100.0
    if total <= 0:
        total = 1100.0
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, total) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = float('inf')

    try:
        raw = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if raw is not None and raw.size >= 20:
            h0 = raw.copy()
            n0 = len(h0)
            if n0 % 2 == 1:
                n0 -= 1
                h0 = h0[:n0]
        else:
            raise Exception("no parent")
    except Exception:
        n0 = 500
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float)

    h0 = exact_project(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0
    best_c = c5_exact(h0)
    nbest = len(best_h)

    try:
        if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
            alloc = min(220.0, max(40.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.25))
            if alloc > 40.0:
                hh, cc = transfer_local_search(best_h, nbest, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                cc = c5_exact(hh)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_c = cc
                    nbest = len(hh)

        if time.time() < deadline - 80.0:
            hb = binarize(best_h, nbest)
            cb = c5_exact(hb)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb
                best_c = cb
            alloc = min(140.0, max(30.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.22))
            if alloc > 30.0:
                hh, cc = transfer_local_search(hb, nbest, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                cc = c5_exact(hh)
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_h = hh
                    best_c = cc
                    nbest = len(hh)

        if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
            alloc = min(90.0, max(25.0, (deadline - time.time()) * 0.18))
            if alloc > 25.0:
                hr, cr = random_transfer_search(best_h, nbest, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                cr = c5_exact(hr)
                if cr < best_c:
                    best_h = hr
                    best_c = cr
                    nbest = len(hr)

        if time.time() < deadline - 160.0 and nbest <= 650:
            for mm in (12, 24, 40):
                if time.time() > deadline - 80.0:
                    break
                counts = lag_counts(best_h)
                top = np.argsort(counts)[-mm:]
                alpha = 40000.0 if mm <= 16 else 18000.0
                maxiter = 70 if nbest <= 500 else 45
                hn = slsqp_active(best_h, nbest, top, alpha, maxiter)
                cn = c5_exact(hn)
                if cn < best_c:
                    best_h = hn
                    best_c = cn
                    nbest = len(hn)
                if time.time() < deadline - 50.0:
                    alloc2 = min(30.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.time() - 50.0) * 0.35))
                    if alloc2 > 10.0:
                        hl, cl = transfer_local_search(best_h, nbest, time.time() + alloc2, rng)
                        cl = c5_exact(hl)
                        if cl < best_c:
                            best_h = hl
                            best_c = cl
                            nbest = len(hl)

        base_for_up = best_h.copy()
        for n_new in (1000, 1400):
            if time.time() > deadline - 70.0:
                break
            remaining = deadline - time.time()
            alloc = min(260.0, remaining * 0.32)
            if alloc < 70.0:
                break

            h_lin = resample_linear(base_for_up, n_new)
            c_try = c5_exact(h_lin)
            if c_try < best_c:
                best_h = h_lin
                best_c = c_try

            h_lin, c_try = transfer_local_search(h_lin, n_new, time.time() + alloc * 0.75, rng)
            c_try = c5_exact(h_lin)
            if c_try < best_c:
                best_h = h_lin
                best_c = c_try
                nbest = n_new

            if time.time() < deadline - 70.0:
                h_near = resample_nearest(base_for_up, n_new)
                c_try = c5_exact(h_near)
                if c_try < best_c:
                    best_h = h_near
                    best_c = c_try
                alloc2 = min(80.0, max(25.0, (deadline - time.time() - 70.0) * 0.30))
                if alloc2 > 25.0:
                    h_near, c_try = transfer_local_search(h_near, n_new, time.time() + alloc2, rng)
                    c_try = c5_exact(h_near)
                    if c_try < best_c:
                        best_h = h_near
                        best_c = c_try
                        nbest = n_new

        if time.time() < deadline - 130.0 and nbest <= 900:
            for scale in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
                if time.time() > deadline - 100.0:
                    break
                hp = perturb_h(best_h, rng, scale)
                alloc = min(45.0, max(15.0, (deadline - time.time() - 100.0) * 0.25))
                if alloc < 15.0:
                    break
                hp, cp = transfer_local_search(hp, nbest, time.time() + alloc, rng)
                cp = c5_exact(hp)
                if cp < best_c:
                    best_h = hp
                    best_c = cp
                    nbest = len(hp)

    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_h is None:
        nbest = 500
        best_h = exact_project(np.full(nbest, 0.5, dtype=float), nbest)
        best_c = c5_exact(best_h)

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        hn, cn = transfer_local_search(best_h, nbest, deadline - 0.5, rng)
        cn = c5_exact(hn)
        if cn < best_c:
            best_h = hn
            best_c = cn
            nbest = len(hn)

    best_h = exact_project(best_h, nbest)
    best_c = c5_exact(best_h)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(nbest)
```
